====================================================================================================
FILE: mods/core-game/scripts/mod.rhai
====================================================================================================
fn on_enter() {
    let active_mod = world.mod.current_id();

    if !world.entities.exists("core-game-mod") {
        world.entities.create("core-game-mod");
    }

    if world.runtime.dev_mode() && !world.entities.exists("core-game-dev-mode") {
        world.entities.create("core-game-dev-mode");
    }

    for mod_id in world.mod.loaded() {
        let marker = "mod:" + mod_id;

        if !world.entities.exists(marker) {
            world.entities.create(marker);
        }
    }

    world.dev.log("core-game mod runtime entered for mod=" + active_mod);
    world.dev.log("window backend=" + world.runtime.window_backend());
    world.dev.log("input backend=" + world.runtime.input_backend());
    world.dev.log("render backend=" + world.runtime.render_backend());
    world.dev.log("script backend=" + world.runtime.script_backend());
    world.dev.log("runtime capabilities=" + world.runtime.capabilities().len);
    world.dev.log("runtime plugins=" + world.runtime.plugins().len);
    world.dev.log("runtime services=" + world.runtime.services().len);
    world.dev.log("assets registered=" + world.assets.registered().len);
    world.dev.log("assets loaded=" + world.assets.loaded().len);
    world.dev.log("assets prepared=" + world.assets.prepared().len);
    world.dev.log("assets failed=" + world.assets.failed().len);
    world.dev.log("assets pending=" + world.assets.pending().len);
    world.dev.log("active mod assets=" + world.assets.by_mod(active_mod).len);

    for scene_id in world.mod.scenes() {
        world.dev.log("available scene for " + active_mod + ": " + scene_id);
    }
}

fn on_event(topic, payload) {
    if topic == "core-game.scene-ready" && payload.len > 0 {
        let scene_id = payload[0];
        let marker = "scene:" + scene_id;

        if !world.entities.exists(marker) {
            world.entities.create(marker);
        }

        world.dev.event("core-game.bootstrapped", scene_id);
        world.dev.log(
            "core-game dev shell bootstrapped for mod="
            + world.mod.current_id()
            + " scene="
            + scene_id
        );
    }

    if topic == "core-game.refresh-diagnostics" && payload.len > 0 {
        world.dev.refresh_diagnostics(payload[0]);
    }

    ()
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: mods/playground-2d/scenes/basic-scripting-demo/scene.rhai
====================================================================================================
import "pkg:amigo.std/input" as input;

let demo_square = world.entities.named("playground-2d-demo-square");
let demo_spritesheet = world.entities.named("playground-2d-demo-spritesheet");

fn on_enter() {
    let available_scenes = world.scene.available();
    let runtime_window = world.runtime.window_backend();
    let runtime_render = world.runtime.render_backend();
    let square_asset = world.assets.get("playground-2d/spritesheets/square");
    let spritesheet_asset = world.assets.get("playground-2d/spritesheets/hello-world-spritesheet");

    world.dev.log("entered basic-scripting-demo for mod=" + world.mod.current_id());
    world.dev.log("runtime=" + runtime_window + "/" + runtime_render);
    world.dev.log("scene-count=" + available_scenes.len);
    world.dev.log("square-asset=" + square_asset.state());
    world.dev.log("spritesheet-asset=" + spritesheet_asset.state());
    world.dev.event("playground-2d.demo.entered", world.scene.current_id());
}

fn update(dt) {
    let speed = 2.0;

    if input::down(world, "demo.move_left") {
        demo_square.rotate_2d(-speed * dt);
    }

    if input::down(world, "demo.move_right") {
        demo_square.rotate_2d(speed * dt);
    }

    if input::pressed(world, "demo.advance_frame") {
        let demo_sheet_name = demo_spritesheet.name();
        world.sprite2d.advance(demo_sheet_name, 1.0 / 8.0);
        let frame = world.sprite2d.frame(demo_sheet_name);
        world.dev.log("basic-scripting-demo frame=" + frame);
    }

    if input::pressed(world, "demo.reset_frame") {
        world.sprite2d.set_frame(demo_spritesheet.name(), 0);
        world.dev.log("basic-scripting-demo frame reset");
    }

    if input::pressed(world, "demo.goto_spritesheet") {
        world.dev.event("playground-2d.demo.goto-spritesheet");
    }

    if input::pressed(world, "demo.reload_scene") {
        world.scene.reload();
    }

    if input::pressed(world, "demo.log_time") {
        let delta = world.time.delta();
        let elapsed = world.time.elapsed();
        let frame = world.time.frame();

        world.dev.log("time-delta=" + delta);
        world.dev.log("time-elapsed=" + elapsed);
        world.dev.log("time-frame=" + frame);
        world.dev.command("help");
    }

    if input::pressed(world, "demo.refresh_diagnostics") {
        world.dev.refresh_diagnostics(world.mod.current_id());
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: mods/playground-2d/scenes/basic-scripting-demo/scene.yml
====================================================================================================
version: 1
scene:
  id: basic-scripting-demo
  label: Scripting Basics
  description: 2D showcase for the world.* Rhai scripting API with input, scene flow, sprite animation, diagnostics, and reload actions.
transitions:
  - id: to-hello-world-spritesheet
    to: hello-world-spritesheet
    when:
      kind: script_event
      topic: playground-2d.demo.goto-spritesheet
entities:
  - id: controls
    name: playground-2d-demo-controls
    components:
      - type: InputActionMap
        id: basic-scripting-demo
        active: true
        actions:
          demo.move_left:
            kind: button
            pressed: [ArrowLeft]
          demo.move_right:
            kind: button
            pressed: [ArrowRight]
          demo.advance_frame:
            kind: button
            pressed: [ArrowUp]
          demo.reset_frame:
            kind: button
            pressed: [ArrowDown]
          demo.goto_square:
            kind: button
            pressed: [KeyW]
          demo.goto_spritesheet:
            kind: button
            pressed: [KeyS]
          demo.reload_scene:
            kind: button
            pressed: [Space]
          demo.log_time:
            kind: button
            pressed: [Enter]
          demo.refresh_diagnostics:
            kind: button
            pressed: [KeyD]
      - type: Behavior
        kind: scene_transition_controller
        action: demo.goto_square
        scene: hello-world-square

  - id: camera
    name: playground-2d-camera
    components:
      - type: Camera2D

  - id: title
    name: playground-2d-demo-title
    transform2:
      translation:
        x: 0.0
        y: 220.0
      rotation_radians: 0.0
      scale:
        x: 1.0
        y: 1.0
    components:

          x: 720.0
          y: 56.0

  - id: controls-1

import "pkg:amigo.std/input" as input;

let spritesheet = world.entities.named("playground-2d-spritesheet");

fn update(dt) {
    world.sprite2d.advance(spritesheet.name(), dt);

    if input::pressed(world, "spritesheet.return_square") {
        world.dev.event("playground-2d.switch.square");
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: mods/playground-2d/scenes/hello-world-square/scene.rhai
====================================================================================================
import "pkg:amigo.std/input" as input;

let square = world.entities.named("playground-2d-square");

fn update(dt) {
    let speed = 2.0;

    if input::down(world, "square.move_left") {
        square.rotate_2d(-speed * dt);
    }

    if input::down(world, "square.move_right") {
        square.rotate_2d(speed * dt);
    }

    ()
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: mods/playground-2d/scenes/screen-space-preview/scene.rhai
====================================================================================================
import "pkg:amigo.std/input" as input;

let square = world.entities.named("playground-2d-ui-preview-square");

let hp = 0.82;
let energy = 0.55;
let threat = 0.35;
let shield_online = true;
let repair_cooldown = 0.0;
let mission_time = 0.0;
let toast_time = 0.0;
let toast_message = "";

fn subtitle_path() { "playground-2d-ui-preview.root.hud-card.subtitle" }
fn hp_bar_path() { "playground-2d-ui-preview.root.hud-card.hp-bar" }
fn energy_bar_path() { "playground-2d-ui-preview.root.hud-card.energy-bar" }
fn threat_bar_path() { "playground-2d-ui-preview.root.hud-card.threat-bar" }
fn shield_value_path() { "playground-2d-ui-preview.root.hud-card.status-row.shield-box.shield-value" }
fn system_value_path() { "playground-2d-ui-preview.root.hud-card.status-row.system-box.system-value" }
fn objective_value_path() { "playground-2d-ui-preview.root.mission-card.objective-value" }
fn phase_value_path() { "playground-2d-ui-preview.root.mission-card.phase-value" }
fn repair_button_path() { "playground-2d-ui-preview.root.control-card.button-row.repair-button" }
fn shield_button_path() { "playground-2d-ui-preview.root.control-card.button-row.shield-button" }
fn stabilize_button_path() { "playground-2d-ui-preview.root.control-card.stabilize-button" }
fn event_toast_path() { "playground-2d-ui-preview.root.event-toast" }
fn event_toast_text_path() { "playground-2d-ui-preview.root.event-toast.event-toast-text" }
fn critical_alert_path() { "playground-2d-ui-preview.root.critical-alert" }

fn clamp01(value) {
    if value < 0.0 { return 0.0; }
    if value > 1.0 { return 1.0; }
    value
}

fn phase_label(current_time) {
    if current_time < 10.0 { return "WAVE 01"; }
    if current_time < 20.0 { return "WAVE 02"; }
    "WAVE 03"
}

fn objective_label(current_hp, shield_active, current_threat) {
    if current_hp < 0.18 { return "OBJECTIVE: STABILIZE HULL"; }
    if !shield_active { return "OBJECTIVE: RESTORE SHIELD"; }
    if current_threat > 0.72 { return "OBJECTIVE: BLEED THREAT"; }
    "OBJECTIVE: SURVIVE CALIBRATION"
}

fn status_label(current_hp, current_threat) {
    if current_hp < 0.18 { return "CRITICAL"; }
    if current_hp < 0.45 || current_threat > 0.75 { return "WARNING"; }
    "STABLE"
}

fn subtitle_label(current_hp, shield_active, current_threat) {
    if current_hp < 0.18 { return "Critical damage detected"; }
    if !shield_active { return "Shield offline - hull exposed"; }
    if current_threat > 0.75 { return "Threat spike detected"; }
    "Rhai combat loop active"
}

fn on_enter() {
    world.dev.event("playground-2d.ui-preview.entered", world.scene.current_id());
    world.ui.hide(event_toast_path());
    world.ui.hide(critical_alert_path());
    world.ui.set_text(subtitle_path(), subtitle_label(hp, shield_online, threat));
    world.ui.set_text(shield_value_path(), if shield_online { "ONLINE" } else { "OFFLINE" });
    world.ui.set_text(system_value_path(), status_label(hp, threat));
    world.ui.set_text(objective_value_path(), objective_label(hp, shield_online, threat));
    world.ui.set_text(phase_value_path(), phase_label(mission_time));
    world.ui.set_text(shield_button_path(), "Disable Shield");
    world.ui.enable(repair_button_path());
    world.ui.set_text(repair_button_path(), "Repair Pulse");
    world.ui.enable(stabilize_button_path());
    world.ui.set_value(hp_bar_path(), hp);
    world.ui.set_value(energy_bar_path(), energy);
    world.ui.set_value(threat_bar_path(), threat);
}

fn update(dt) {
    mission_time = mission_time + dt;
    square.rotate_2d(dt * (0.45 + threat));

    threat = clamp01(threat + dt * 0.04);
    energy = clamp01(energy + dt * 0.06);

    if shield_online {
        energy = clamp01(energy - dt * 0.02);
    }

    if !shield_online && threat > 0.6 {
        hp = clamp01(hp - dt * 0.1);
    }

    if threat > 0.85 {
        hp = clamp01(hp - dt * 0.05);
    }

    let move_x = input::axis(world, "preview.move_x");
    let move_y = input::axis(world, "preview.move_y");

    if move_x < -0.01 {
        hp = clamp01(hp - dt * 0.3);
    }

    if move_x > 0.01 {
        hp = clamp01(hp + dt * 0.2);
    }

    if move_y > 0.01 {
        energy = clamp01(energy + dt * 0.3);
    }

    if move_y < -0.01 {
        threat = clamp01(threat - dt * 0.3);
    }

    if input::pressed(world, "preview.repair") {
        if repair_cooldown > 0.0 {
            toast_message = "REPAIR COOLING";
            toast_time = 0.9;
        } else if energy < 0.2 {
            toast_message = "NOT ENOUGH ENERGY";
            toast_time = 0.9;
        } else {
            hp = clamp01(hp + 0.24);
            energy = clamp01(energy - 0.2);
            threat = clamp01(threat - 0.08);
            repair_cooldown = 2.4;
            toast_message = "REPAIR PULSE ACTIVATED";
            toast_time = 1.2;
        }
    }

    if input::pressed(world, "preview.toggle_shield") {
        shield_online = !shield_online;
        if shield_online {
            toast_message = "SHIELD ONLINE";
            toast_time = 1.0;
        } else {
            toast_message = "SHIELD OFFLINE";
            toast_time = 1.0;
        }
    }

    if input::pressed(world, "preview.stabilize") {
        if energy < 0.15 {
            toast_message = "CAPACITOR TOO LOW";
            toast_time = 0.9;
        } else {
            energy = clamp01(energy - 0.15);
            threat = clamp01(threat - 0.26);
            toast_message = "THREAT DAMPENED";
            toast_time = 1.0;
        }
    }

    let had_cooldown = repair_cooldown > 0.0;
    if repair_cooldown > 0.0 {
        repair_cooldown = repair_cooldown - dt;
        if repair_cooldown < 0.0 {
            repair_cooldown = 0.0;
        }
    }

    if had_cooldown && repair_cooldown == 0.0 {
        toast_message = "REPAIR READY";
        toast_time = 0.8;
    }

    if toast_time > 0.0 {
        toast_time = toast_time - dt;
        if toast_time < 0.0 {
            toast_time = 0.0;
            toast_message = "";
        }
    }

    world.ui.set_text(subtitle_path(), subtitle_label(hp, shield_online, threat));
    world.ui.set_text(shield_value_path(), if shield_online { "ONLINE" } else { "OFFLINE" });
    world.ui.set_text(system_value_path(), status_label(hp, threat));
    world.ui.set_text(objective_value_path(), objective_label(hp, shield_online, threat));
    world.ui.set_text(phase_value_path(), phase_label(mission_time));

    if shield_online {
        world.ui.set_text(shield_button_path(), "Disable Shield");
    } else {
        world.ui.set_text(shield_button_path(), "Enable Shield");
    }

    if repair_cooldown > 0.0 {
        world.ui.disable(repair_button_path());
        world.ui.set_text(repair_button_path(), "Repair Cooling");
    } else {
        world.ui.enable(repair_button_path());
        world.ui.set_text(repair_button_path(), "Repair Pulse");
    }

    if energy < 0.15 {
        world.ui.disable(stabilize_button_path());
    } else {
        world.ui.enable(stabilize_button_path());
    }

    if hp < 0.18 {
        world.ui.show(critical_alert_path());
    } else {
        world.ui.hide(critical_alert_path());
    }

    if toast_time > 0.0 {
        world.ui.set_text(event_toast_text_path(), toast_message);
        world.ui.show(event_toast_path());
    } else {
        world.ui.hide(event_toast_path());
    }

    world.ui.set_value(hp_bar_path(), hp);
    world.ui.set_value(energy_bar_path(), energy);
    world.ui.set_value(threat_bar_path(), threat);
}

fn on_event(topic, payload) {
    if topic == "playground-2d.ui-preview.repair-clicked" {
        if repair_cooldown > 0.0 {
            toast_message = "REPAIR COOLING";
            toast_time = 0.9;
        } else if energy < 0.2 {
            toast_message = "NOT ENOUGH ENERGY";
            toast_time = 0.9;
        } else {
            hp = clamp01(hp + 0.24);
            energy = clamp01(energy - 0.2);
            threat = clamp01(threat - 0.08);
            repair_cooldown = 2.4;
            toast_message = "REPAIR PULSE ACTIVATED";
            toast_time = 1.2;
        }
    }

    if topic == "playground-2d.ui-preview.shield-clicked" {
        shield_online = !shield_online;
        if shield_online {
            toast_message = "SHIELD ONLINE";
            toast_time = 1.0;
        } else {
            toast_message = "SHIELD OFFLINE";
            toast_time = 1.0;
        }
    }

    if topic == "playground-2d.ui-preview.stabilize-clicked" {
        if energy < 0.15 {
            toast_message = "CAPACITOR TOO LOW";
            toast_time = 0.9;
        } else {
            energy = clamp01(energy - 0.15);
            threat = clamp01(threat - 0.26);
            toast_message = "THREAT DAMPENED";
            toast_time = 1.0;
        }
    }

    world.ui.set_text(subtitle_path(), subtitle_label(hp, shield_online, threat));
    world.ui.set_text(shield_value_path(), if shield_online { "ONLINE" } else { "OFFLINE" });
    world.ui.set_text(system_value_path(), status_label(hp, threat));
    world.ui.set_text(objective_value_path(), objective_label(hp, shield_online, threat));
    world.ui.set_text(phase_value_path(), phase_label(mission_time));

    if shield_online {
        world.ui.set_text(shield_button_path(), "Disable Shield");
    } else {
        world.ui.set_text(shield_button_path(), "Enable Shield");
    }

    if repair_cooldown > 0.0 {
        world.ui.disable(repair_button_path());
        world.ui.set_text(repair_button_path(), "Repair Cooling");
    } else {
        world.ui.enable(repair_button_path());
        world.ui.set_text(repair_button_path(), "Repair Pulse");
    }

    if energy < 0.15 {
        world.ui.disable(stabilize_button_path());
    } else {
        world.ui.enable(stabilize_button_path());
    }

    if hp < 0.18 {
        world.ui.show(critical_alert_path());
    } else {
        world.ui.hide(critical_alert_path());
    }

    if toast_time > 0.0 {
        world.ui.set_text(event_toast_text_path(), toast_message);
        world.ui.show(event_toast_path());
    } else {
        world.ui.hide(event_toast_path());
    }

    world.ui.set_value(hp_bar_path(), hp);
    world.ui.set_value(energy_bar_path(), energy);
    world.ui.set_value(threat_bar_path(), threat);
}

FILE: mods/playground-2d/scripts/components/lifecycle_probe.rhai
====================================================================================================
fn on_attach(entity, params) {
    world.state.set_string(entity + ".component.label", params.label);
    world.state.set_float(entity + ".component.elapsed", 0.0);
    world.dev.event("playground-2d.demo.component.attach", entity);
}

fn update(entity, params, dt) {
    let elapsed_key = entity + ".component.elapsed";
    let elapsed = world.state.get_float(elapsed_key);
    world.state.set_float(elapsed_key, elapsed + dt * params.speed);
}

fn on_detach(entity, params) {
    world.dev.event("playground-2d.demo.component.detach", entity);
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: mods/playground-2d-asteroids/README.md
====================================================================================================
# Asteroids

Procedural, vector-first 2D playground mod for early Asteroids-style engine work.

Current scope:

- native `vector_2d` scene components
- WGPU stroke/fill rendering for simple vector shapes
- generated synthetic arcade SFX for shot, thrust, asteroid hits, wave progression, respawn protection, and game over cues
- lightweight runtime-owned `CircleCollider2D` overlap queries exposed through `world.physics`
- runtime vector mutation API exposed through `world.vector` for procedural shape updates
- no sprite assets
- no world wrap yet
- no full rigid-body or wrap-around gameplay physics yet

The first slice is intentionally small and exists to validate:

1. scene YAML -> vector hydration
2. runtime scene queueing
3. vector rendering in hosted mode
4. first playable ship / bullet / asteroid loop without bitmap assets
5. thin collision seam from scene components into Rhai through `world.physics.overlaps(...)`
6. first bounded two-wave arena flow before world-wrap work

Audio notes:

- all Asteroids SFX in this mod are procedural `generated-audio` metadata assets
- `thrust.yml` adds a soft low-frequency engine pulse intended for short boost/thrust accents
- `wave-start.yml` is a short upward cue for spawning/announcing the next wave
- `sector-clear.yml` is a brighter progression sting for clearing the current arena slice
- `respawn-shield.yml` is a soft protective cue for ship respawn / temporary invulnerability beats

Scene polish notes:

- the arena now uses a full black vector backdrop and a larger field/frame footprint tuned for full-window readability
- the HUD copy is tuned to feel a bit more arcade-like while keeping the same ids and script wiring
- the scene now runs a real second wave using additional off-screen-staged asteroid entities instead of stopping after the first clear
- asteroid silhouettes are generated procedurally in script and pushed into runtime via `world.vector.set_polygon(...)`


====================================================================================================
FILE: mods/playground-2d-asteroids/scenes/game/scene.rhai
====================================================================================================
import "pkg:amigo.std/input" as input;

fn ship_name() { "playground-2d-asteroids-ship" }
fn shield_name() { "playground-2d-asteroids-ship-shield" }
fn thrust_name() { "playground-2d-asteroids-main-thruster" }
fn starfield_name() { "playground-2d-asteroids-speed-starfield" }
fn bullet_pool() { "player-bullets" }
fn asteroid_pool() { "asteroids" }

fn game_over_name() { "playground-2d-asteroids-game-over" }
fn hud_name() { "playground-2d-asteroids-hud" }
fn status_name() { "playground-2d-asteroids-center-status" }
fn ui_root(entity_name) { entity_name + ".root" }

fn shield_seconds() { 3.0 }
fn wave_clear_delay() { 1.2 }
fn status_flash_seconds() { 0.95 }
fn ship_spawn_rotation() { 1.5707963 }

fn mode(world_api) { world_api.state.get_string("game_mode") }
fn set_mode(world_api, value) { world_api.state.set_string("game_mode", value); }
fn score(world_api) { world_api.state.get_int("score") }
fn set_score(world_api, value) { world_api.state.set_int("score", value); }
fn lives(world_api) { world_api.state.get_int("lives") }
fn set_lives(world_api, value) { world_api.state.set_int("lives", value); }
fn wave(world_api) { world_api.state.get_int("wave") }
fn set_wave(world_api, value) { world_api.state.set_int("wave", value); }
fn low_mode(world_api) { world_api.session.get_bool("asteroids.low_mode") }
fn rng_seed(world_api) { world_api.state.get_int("rng_seed") }
fn set_rng_seed(world_api, value) { world_api.state.set_int("rng_seed", value); }

fn pad_two(value) {
    if value < 10 { return "0" + value; }
    return "" + value;
}

fn pad_score(value) {
    if value < 10 { return "00000" + value; }
    if value < 100 { return "0000" + value; }
    if value < 1000 { return "000" + value; }
    if value < 10000 { return "00" + value; }
    if value < 100000 { return "0" + value; }
    return "" + value;
}

fn clamp_int(value, min_value, max_value) {
    if value < min_value { return min_value; }
    if value > max_value { return max_value; }
    return value;
}

fn max_float(left, right) {
    if left > right { return left; }
    return right;
}

fn abs_float(value) {
    if value < 0.0 { return -value; }
    return value;
}

fn clamp_float(value, min_value, max_value) {
    if value < min_value { return min_value; }
    if value > max_value { return max_value; }
    return value;
}

fn thrust_hold(world_api) { world_api.state.get_float("ship.thrust_hold"); }
fn set_thrust_hold(world_api, value) { world_api.state.set_float("ship.thrust_hold", value); }

fn rand_next(world_api) {
    let next = (rng_seed(world_api) * 1664525 + 1013904223) % 4294967296;
    if next < 0 { next = -next; }
    set_rng_seed(world_api, next);
    return next;
}

fn rand01(world_api) {
    return rand_next(world_api) / 4294967296.0;
}

fn rand_range(world_api, min_value, max_value) {
    return min_value + (max_value - min_value) * rand01(world_api);
}

fn rand_int(world_api, min_value, max_value) {
    let span = max_value - min_value + 1;
    return min_value + (rand_next(world_api) % span);
}

fn sync_hud(world_api) {
    world_api.state.set_string("score_text", pad_score(score(world_api)));
    world_api.state.set_string("lives_text", "LIVES " + lives(world_api));
    world_api.state.set_string("wave_text", "WAVE " + pad_two(wave(world_api)));
    world_api.state.set_string("final_score_text", "SCORE " + pad_score(score(world_api)));
}

fn set_status(world_api, value) {
    world_api.state.set_bool("status_auto_clear", false);
    world_api.state.set_string("status_text", value);
    if value == "" {
        world_api.entities.hide(status_name());
        world_api.ui.hide(ui_root(status_name()));
    } else {
        world_api.entities.show(status_name());
        world_api.ui.show(ui_root(status_name()));
    }
}

fn set_timed_status(world_api, value, seconds) {
    set_status(world_api, value);
    world_api.state.set_bool("status_auto_clear", true);
    world_api.timers.start("status-clear", seconds);
}

fn update_status(world_api) {
    if world_api.state.get_bool("status_auto_clear") && world_api.timers.ready("status-clear") {
        world_api.state.set_bool("status_auto_clear", false);
        set_status(world_api, "");
    }
}

fn show_gameplay_ui(world_api) {
    world_api.entities.hide(game_over_name());
    world_api.ui.hide(ui_root(game_over_name()));
    world_api.entities.show(hud_name());
    world_api.ui.show(ui_root(hud_name()));
}

fn highscore(world_api, index) {
    return world_api.session.get_int("asteroids.highscore." + index);
}

fn set_highscore(world_api, index, value) {
    world_api.session.set_int("asteroids.highscore." + index, value);
}

fn maybe_insert_highscore(world_api, value) {
    if value > highscore(world_api, 1) {
        set_highscore(world_api, 5, highscore(world_api, 4));
        set_highscore(world_api, 4, highscore(world_api, 3));
        set_highscore(world_api, 3, highscore(world_api, 2));
        set_highscore(world_api, 2, highscore(world_api, 1));
        set_highscore(world_api, 1, value);
        return;
    }
    if value > highscore(world_api, 2) {
        set_highscore(world_api, 5, highscore(world_api, 4));
        set_highscore(world_api, 4, highscore(world_api, 3));
        set_highscore(world_api, 3, highscore(world_api, 2));
        set_highscore(world_api, 2, value);
        return;
    }
    if value > highscore(world_api, 3) {
        set_highscore(world_api, 5, highscore(world_api, 4));
        set_highscore(world_api, 4, highscore(world_api, 3));
        set_highscore(world_api, 3, value);
        return;
    }
    if value > highscore(world_api, 4) {
        set_highscore(world_api, 5, highscore(world_api, 4));
        set_highscore(world_api, 4, value);
        return;
    }
    if value > highscore(world_api, 5) {
        set_highscore(world_api, 5, value);
    }
}

fn reset_player(world_api) {
    world_api.entities.set_position_2d(ship_name(), 0.0, 0.0);
    world_api.entities.set_position_2d(shield_name(), 0.0, 0.0);
    world_api.entities.set_rotation_2d(ship_name(), ship_spawn_rotation());
    world_api.entities.set_rotation_2d(shield_name(), ship_spawn_rotation());
    world_api.motion.reset_freeflight(ship_name(), ship_spawn_rotation());
    world_api.motion.reset_freeflight(shield_name(), ship_spawn_rotation());
    world_api.motion.set_velocity(ship_name(), 0.0, 0.0);
    world_api.motion.set_velocity(shield_name(), 0.0, 0.0);
    world_api.particles.set_active(thrust_name(), false);
    world_api.particles.set_intensity(thrust_name(), 0.0);
    world_api.particles.set_active(starfield_name(), false);
    world_api.particles.set_intensity(starfield_name(), 0.0);
    set_thrust_hold(world_api, 0.0);
    world_api.entities.show(ship_name());
    world_api.entities.enable(ship_name());
    world_api.entities.set_collision_enabled(ship_name(), true);
    world_api.entities.show(shield_name());
    world_api.timers.start("ship-shield", shield_seconds());
}

fn start_new_game(world_api) {
    world_api.timers.reset_scene();
    set_score(world_api, 0);
    set_lives(world_api, 3);
    set_wave(world_api, 1);
    set_rng_seed(world_api, 1337);
    if low_mode(world_api) {
        set_rng_seed(world_api, 7331);
    }

    world_api.pools.release_all(bullet_pool());
    world_api.pools.release_all(asteroid_pool());
    show_gameplay_ui(world_api);
    sync_hud(world_api);
    set_timed_status(world_api, "READY", status_flash_seconds());
    reset_player(world_api);
    set_mode(world_api, "playing");
    start_wave(world_api, 1);
    world_api.audio.play("wave-start");
}

fn wave_asteroid_count(world_api, current_wave) {
    let base = 3 + current_wave;
    if low_mode(world_api) {
        base = base - 1;
    }
    return clamp_int(base, 3, 16);
}

fn wave_speed_min(world_api, current_wave) {
    let value = 55.0 + current_wave * 8.0;
    if low_mode(world_api) {
        return value * 0.72;
    }
    return value;
}

fn wave_speed_max(world_api, current_wave) {
    let value = 110.0 + current_wave * 12.0;
    if low_mode(world_api) {
        return value * 0.72;
    }
    return value;
}

fn wave_radius_min(current_wave) {
    return max_float(14.0, 34.0 - current_wave * 1.2);
}

fn wave_radius_max() { 44.0 }

fn score_for_radius(radius) {
    if radius >= 34.0 { return 20; }
    if radius >= 22.0 { return 50; }
    return 100;
}

fn smooth_step(value) {
    let t = clamp_float(value, 0.0, 1.0);
    return t * t * (3.0 - 2.0 * t);
}

fn ease_in_out_cubic(value) {
    let t = clamp_float(value, 0.0, 1.0);
    if t < 0.5 {
        return 4.0 * t * t * t;
    }
    let f = -2.0 * t + 2.0;
    return 1.0 - f * f * f / 2.0;
}

fn update_speed_starfield(world_api, thrust_axis, dt) {
    if thrust_axis <= 0.01 {
        set_thrust_hold(world_api, 0.0);
    } else {
        set_thrust_hold(world_api, clamp_float(thrust_hold(world_api) + dt, 0.0, 5.0));
    }

    let intensity = clamp_float((thrust_axis - 0.82) / 0.18, 0.0, 1.0);
    let ramp_raw = clamp_float((thrust_hold(world_api) - 0.72) / 1.35, 0.0, 1.0);
    let ramp = ease_in_out_cubic(ramp_raw);
    let streak = smooth_step(ramp * ramp);
    let motion = world_api.motion.state(ship_name());
    let speed_sq = motion.velocity_x * motion.velocity_x + motion.velocity_y * motion.velocity_y;

    if intensity <= 0.01 || ramp <= 0.01 || speed_sq < 115000.0 {
        world_api.particles.set_active(starfield_name(), false);
        world_api.particles.set_intensity(starfield_name(), 0.0);
        return;
    }

    let chaos_phase = (thrust_hold(world_api) * 21.0).to_int() % 4;
    let chaos = 0.0;
    if chaos_phase == 0 { chaos = 1.0 * ramp; }
    if chaos_phase == 1 { chaos = 0.45 * ramp; }

    world_api.particles.set_active(starfield_name(), true);
    world_api.particles.set_intensity(starfield_name(), intensity * ramp);
    world_api.particles.set_local_direction_degrees(starfield_name(), motion.angle_radians * 57.29578 + 180.0);
    world_api.particles.set_spawn_rate(starfield_name(), 32.0 + 112.0 * intensity * ramp + 42.0 * chaos);
    world_api.particles.set_lifetime(starfield_name(), 0.26 + 0.42 * ramp + 0.035 * chaos);
    world_api.particles.set_lifetime_jitter(starfield_name(), 0.06 + 0.045 * chaos);
    world_api.particles.set_speed(starfield_name(), 320.0 + 380.0 * intensity * ramp + 72.0 * chaos);
    world_api.particles.set_speed_jitter(starfield_name(), 44.0 + 92.0 * ramp + 32.0 * chaos);
    world_api.particles.set_spread_degrees(starfield_name(), 1.2 + 1.4 * chaos);
    world_api.particles.set_initial_size(starfield_name(), 0.16 + 0.18 * ramp);
    world_api.particles.set_final_size(starfield_name(), 0.55 + 1.35 * streak + 0.35 * chaos);
    world_api.particles.set_shape_line(starfield_name(), 3.0 + 20.0 * streak + 4.0 * chaos);
}

fn split_radius(radius) {
    if radius >= 34.0 { return radius * 0.58; }
    if radius >= 22.0 { return radius * 0.62; }
    return 0.0;
}

fn asteroid_size_for_radius(radius) {
    if radius >= 34.0 { return "large"; }
    if radius >= 22.0 { return "medium"; }
    return "small";
}

fn fragment_speed(world_api, parent_speed) {
    let min_speed = wave_speed_min(world_api, wave(world_api)) * 1.15;
    let speed = parent_speed * 1.18;
    return max_float(min_speed, speed);
}

fn random_spawn_position_away_from_center(world_api) {
    let origin = world_api.entities.position_2d(ship_name());
    let x = rand_range(world_api, -860.0, 860.0);
    let y = rand_range(world_api, -460.0, 460.0);

    if abs_float(x) < 220.0 && abs_float(y) < 180.0 {
        if abs_float(x) < abs_float(y) {
            if y >= 0.0 { y = 260.0; } else { y = -260.0; }
        } else {
            if x >= 0.0 { x = 300.0; } else { x = -300.0; }
        }
    }

    return #{ x: origin.x + x, y: origin.y + y };
}

fn direction_x(index) {
    if index == 0 { return 1.0; }
    if index == 1 { return 0.7071; }
    if index == 2 { return 0.0; }
    if index == 3 { return -0.7071; }
    if index == 4 { return -1.0; }
    if index == 5 { return -0.7071; }
    if index == 6 { return 0.0; }
    return 0.7071;
}

fn direction_y(index) {
    if index == 0 { return 0.0; }
    if index == 1 { return 0.7071; }
    if index == 2 { return 1.0; }
    if index == 3 { return 0.7071; }
    if index == 4 { return 0.0; }
    if index == 5 { return -0.7071; }
    if index == 6 { return -1.0; }
    return -0.7071;
}

fn configure_asteroid(world_api, asteroid, x, y, radius, vertices, speed, direction, seed) {
    world_api.entities.set_position_2d(asteroid, x, y);
    world_api.motion.set_velocity(
        asteroid,
        direction_x(direction) * speed,
        direction_y(direction) * speed,
    );
    world_api.vector.set_radial_jitter_polygon(asteroid, vertices, radius, 0.24, seed);
    world_api.physics.set_circle_radius(asteroid, radius);
    world_api.entities.set_property_string(asteroid, "asteroid_size", asteroid_size_for_radius(radius));
    world_api.entities.set_property_int(asteroid, "score_value", score_for_radius(radius));
    world_api.entities.set_property_float(asteroid, "radius", radius);
    world_api.entities.set_property_float(asteroid, "speed", speed);
}

fn start_wave(world_api, current_wave) {
    world_api.pools.release_all(asteroid_pool());
    let count = wave_asteroid_count(world_api, current_wave);
    let i = 0;

    while i < count {
        let asteroid = world_api.pools.acquire(asteroid_pool());
        if asteroid == "" { return; }

        let radius = rand_range(world_api, wave_radius_min(current_wave), wave_radius_max());
        let vertices = rand_int(world_api, 8, 14);
        let speed = rand_range(world_api, wave_speed_min(world_api, current_wave), wave_speed_max(world_api, current_wave));
        let direction = rand_int(world_api, 0, 7);
        let pos = random_spawn_position_away_from_center(world_api);
        let seed = rng_seed(world_api) + i;

        configure_asteroid(
            world_api,
            asteroid,
            pos.x,
            pos.y,
            radius,
            vertices,
            speed,
            direction,
            seed,
        );

        i = i + 1;
    }

    set_wave(world_api, current_wave);
    sync_hud(world_api);
    set_timed_status(world_api, "WAVE " + pad_two(current_wave), status_flash_seconds());
}

fn enter_wave_clear(world_api) {
    set_mode(world_api, "wave_clear");
    world_api.particles.set_active(thrust_name(), false);
    world_api.particles.set_intensity(thrust_name(), 0.0);
    world_api.particles.set_active(starfield_name(), false);
    world_api.particles.set_intensity(starfield_name(), 0.0);
    set_score(world_api, score(world_api) + wave(world_api) * 100);
    sync_hud(world_api);
    set_status(world_api, "WAVE CLEAR");
    world_api.timers.start("wave-clear", wave_clear_delay());
    world_api.audio.play("wave-start");
}

fn update_wave_clear(world_api) {
    if world_api.timers.ready("wave-clear") {
        let next_wave = wave(world_api) + 1;
        set_mode(world_api, "playing");
        start_wave(world_api, next_wave);
        world_api.audio.play("wave-start");
    }
}

fn enter_game_over(world_api) {
    set_mode(world_api, "game_over");
    world_api.pools.release_all(bullet_pool());
    world_api.pools.release_all(asteroid_pool());
    world_api.entities.hide(ship_name());
    world_api.entities.disable(ship_name());
    world_api.entities.set_collision_enabled(ship_name(), false);
    world_api.particles.set_active(thrust_name(), false);
    world_api.particles.set_intensity(thrust_name(), 0.0);
    world_api.particles.set_active(starfield_name(), false);
    world_api.particles.set_intensity(starfield_name(), 0.0);
    world_api.entities.hide(shield_name());
    maybe_insert_highscore(world_api, score(world_api));
    sync_hud(world_api);
    world_api.entities.hide(hud_name());
    world_api.ui.hide(ui_root(hud_name()));
    world_api.entities.show(game_over_name());
    world_api.ui.show(ui_root(game_over_name()));
    set_status(world_api, "");
    world_api.audio.play("game-over");
}

fn update_game_over(world_api) {}

fn update_shield(world_api) {
    if world_api.timers.active("ship-shield") {
        world_api.entities.show(shield_name());
    } else {
        world_api.entities.hide(shield_name());
    }
}

fn update_player_input(world_api, dt) {
    let thrust_axis = input::axis(world_api, "ship.thrust");
    update_speed_starfield(world_api, thrust_axis, dt);
}

fn update_playing(world_api, dt) {
    if input::pressed(world_api, "game.restart") {
        start_new_game(world_api);
        return;
    }

    update_player_input(world_api, dt);
    update_shield(world_api);
    update_status(world_api);
}

fn spawn_split_fragment(world_api, x, y, radius, speed, direction, seed) {
    let fragment = world_api.pools.acquire(asteroid_pool());
    if fragment == "" { return false; }

    let offset = radius * 0.55;
    configure_asteroid(
        world_api,
        fragment,
        x + direction_x(direction) * offset,
        y + direction_y(direction) * offset,
        radius,
        rand_int(world_api, 8, 12),
        speed,
        direction,
        seed,
    );
    return true;
}

fn split_asteroid(world_api, asteroid, radius, x, y) {
    let child_radius = split_radius(radius);
    if child_radius <= 0.0 { return 0; }

    let parent_speed = world_api.entities.property_float(asteroid, "speed");
    let speed = fragment_speed(world_api, parent_speed);
    let direction = rand_int(world_api, 0, 7);
    let opposite = (direction + 4) % 8;
    let seed = rng_seed(world_api);
    let spawned = 0;

    if spawn_split_fragment(world_api, x, y, child_radius, speed, direction, seed + 17) {
        spawned = spawned + 1;
    }
    if spawn_split_fragment(world_api, x, y, child_radius, speed, opposite, seed + 31) {
        spawned = spawned + 1;
    }

    return spawned;
}

fn handle_bullet_hit(world_api, bullet, asteroid) {
    let pos = world_api.entities.position_2d(asteroid);
    let radius = world_api.entities.property_float(asteroid, "radius");
    let score_value = world_api.entities.property_int(asteroid, "score_value");

    world_api.pools.release(bullet_pool(), bullet);
    set_score(world_api, score(world_api) + score_value);
    world_api.pools.release(asteroid_pool(), asteroid);
    split_asteroid(world_api, asteroid, radius, pos.x, pos.y);
    world_api.audio.play("asteroid-hit");
    sync_hud(world_api);

    if world_api.pools.active_count(asteroid_pool()) <= 0 {
        enter_wave_clear(world_api);
    }
}

fn handle_ship_hit(world_api) {
    if mode(world_api) != "playing" { return; }
    if world_api.timers.active("ship-shield") { return; }

    set_lives(world_api, lives(world_api) - 1);
    sync_hud(world_api);
    world_api.pools.release_all(bullet_pool());

    if lives(world_api) <= 0 {
        enter_game_over(world_api);
        return;
    }

    set_timed_status(world_api, "READY", status_flash_seconds());
    reset_player(world_api);
    world_api.audio.play("respawn-shield");
}

fn on_enter() {
    world.state.reset_scene();
    world.timers.reset_scene();

    let clips = [
        "shot",
        "asteroid-hit",
        "game-over",
        "wave-start",
        "respawn-shield",
    ];
    let clip_index = 0;
    while clip_index < clips.len() {
        world.audio.preload(clips[clip_index]);
        clip_index = clip_index + 1;
    }

    start_new_game(world);
}

fn on_event(topic, payload) {
    if topic == "asteroids.bullet_hit_asteroid" && payload.len() >= 2 {
        if mode(world) == "playing" {
            handle_bullet_hit(world, payload[0], payload[1]);
        }
        return;
    }

    if topic == "asteroids.ship_hit_asteroid" {
        handle_ship_hit(world);
        return;
    }
}

fn update(dt) {
    let current_mode = mode(world);

    if current_mode == "playing" {
        update_playing(world, dt);
        return;
    }

    if current_mode == "wave_clear" {
        update_wave_clear(world);
        return;
    }

    if current_mode == "game_over" {
        update_game_over(world);
        return;
    }

}

FILE: mods/playground-2d-asteroids/scenes/highscores/scene.rhai
====================================================================================================
fn pad_score(value) {
    if value < 10 { return "00000" + value; }
    if value < 100 { return "0000" + value; }
    if value < 1000 { return "000" + value; }
    if value < 10000 { return "00" + value; }
    if value < 100000 { return "0" + value; }
    return "" + value;
}

fn highscore(world_api, index) {
    let value = world_api.session.get_int("asteroids.highscore." + index);
    if value > 0 { return value; }
    if index == 1 { return 10000; }
    if index == 2 { return 7500; }
    if index == 3 { return 5000; }
    if index == 4 { return 2500; }
    return 1000;
}

fn sync_highscores(world_api) {
    let text = "AAA " + pad_score(highscore(world_api, 1))
        + "\nBBB " + pad_score(highscore(world_api, 2))
        + "\nCCC " + pad_score(highscore(world_api, 3))
        + "\nDDD " + pad_score(highscore(world_api, 4))
        + "\nEEE " + pad_score(highscore(world_api, 5));
    world_api.state.set_string("highscores_text", text);
}

fn on_enter() {
    world.state.reset_scene();
    sync_highscores(world);
}

fn update(dt) {}

====================================================================================================
FILE: mods/playground-2d-asteroids/scenes/main-menu/scene.rhai
====================================================================================================
fn menu_label(world_api, index, label) {
    return label;
}

fn seed_highscores(world_api) {
    if world_api.session.get_int("asteroids.highscore.1") <= 0 {
        world_api.session.set_int("asteroids.highscore.1", 10000);
        world_api.session.set_int("asteroids.highscore.2", 7500);
        world_api.session.set_int("asteroids.highscore.3", 5000);
        world_api.session.set_int("asteroids.highscore.4", 2500);
        world_api.session.set_int("asteroids.highscore.5", 1000);
    }
}

fn sync_menu(world_api) {
    world_api.state.set_string("menu_start_text", menu_label(world_api, 0, "START"));
    world_api.state.set_string("menu_options_text", menu_label(world_api, 1, "OPTIONS"));
    world_api.state.set_string("menu_highscores_text", menu_label(world_api, 2, "HIGHSCORES"));
    world_api.state.set_string("menu_quit_text", menu_label(world_api, 3, "QUIT"));
}

fn on_enter() {
    world.state.reset_scene();
    world.state.set_int("menu_index", 0);
    world.state.set_string("status_text", "");
    world.state.set_string("menu_color.0", "#FFFFFFFF");
    world.state.set_string("menu_color.1", "#9A9A9AFF");
    world.state.set_string("menu_color.2", "#9A9A9AFF");
    world.state.set_string("menu_color.3", "#9A9A9AFF");
    seed_highscores(world);
    world.audio.preload("menu-move");
    world.audio.preload("menu-select");
    sync_menu(world);
}

fn update(dt) {}

====================================================================================================
FILE: mods/playground-2d-asteroids/scenes/options/scene.rhai
====================================================================================================
fn low_mode(world_api) { world_api.session.get_bool("asteroids.low_mode") }

fn sync_options(world_api) {
    let low = "LOW: OFF";
    if low_mode(world_api) {
        low = "LOW: ON";
    }
    world_api.state.set_string("option_low_text", low);
    world_api.state.set_string("option_back_text", "BACK");
}

fn on_enter() {
    world.state.reset_scene();
    world.state.set_int("option_index", 0);
    world.state.set_string("option_color.0", "#FFFFFFFF");
    world.state.set_string("option_color.1", "#9A9A9AFF");
    world.audio.preload("menu-move");
    world.audio.preload("menu-select");
    sync_options(world);
}

fn on_event(topic, payload) {
    if topic == "asteroids.options.toggle-low" {
        world.session.set_bool("asteroids.low_mode", !low_mode(world));
        sync_options(world);
    }
}

fn update(dt) {}

====================================================================================================
FILE: mods/playground-2d-particles/presets/README.md
====================================================================================================
# Particle Presets

Preset files are the source of truth for showcase particle variants.

At app bootstrap the runtime scans loaded mods for `presets/*.yml`, registers every `kind: particle-preset-2d` document, and exposes the catalog through `world.particles.preset_ids()` / `world.particles.apply_preset(...)`. The showcase scene keeps one `preview-emitter` in `scenes/showcase/scene.yml`, fills the preset dropdown with `world.ui.set_options(...)`, and applies selected preset data from this catalog at runtime. The editor scene uses the same registry-backed dropdown as a starting point before manual parameter edits.

The catalog currently mirrors the showcase presets:

- `fire`
- `smoke`
- `sparks`
- `magic`
- `snow`
- `dust`
- `engine_plume`
- `plasma`
- `portal`
- `rain`
- `explosion`
- `embers`
- `steam`
- `lightning`
- `healing`
- `poison`
- `starfield`
- `fountain`
- `shockwave`
- `aurora`
- `bubbles`
- `fireflies`
- `frost`
- `lava_sparks`
- `muzzle_flash`
- `pollen`
- `sandstorm`
- `spiral`
- `waterfall`
- `welding`

`editor-export.example.yml` shows the shape emitted by the editor `Export` action and accepted by the preset loader. Runtime exports are written to `target/amigo-dev-exports/particle-presets/<preset>-edited.yml`.

## Format

```yaml
kind: particle-preset-2d
id: plasma
label: Plasma
category: energy
tags: [continuous, energy]
emitter:
  type: ParticleEmitter2D
  spawn_rate: 150.0
  max_particles: 140
```

The editor `Export` tab prints and writes this same wrapper shape:

```yaml
kind: particle-preset-2d
id: editor-export
label: Editor Export
category: custom
tags: [editor, custom]
emitter:
  type: ParticleEmitter2D
  active: true
  spawn_rate: 90.0
  max_particles: 160
  particle_lifetime: 0.65
  initial_speed: 100.0
  spread_degrees: 28.0
  color_ramp:
    interpolation: linear_rgb
    stops:
      - { t: 0.0, color: "#FFFFFFFF" }
      - { t: 0.25, color: "#39D7FFFF" }
      - { t: 0.70, color: "#236DFFFF" }
      - { t: 1.0, color: "#0A1A6600" }
```

====================================================================================================
FILE: mods/playground-2d-particles/scenes/editor/scene.rhai
====================================================================================================
import "pkg:amigo.std/input" as input;

fn emitter() { "playground-2d-particles-editor-preview-emitter" }
fn root() { "playground-2d-particles-editor-ui.root" }
fn path(id) { root() + "." + id }
fn controls_path(id) { path("main.controls." + id) }
fn panel_path(id) { controls_path("tab-panels." + id) }
fn emission_path(id) { panel_path("emission-panel." + id) }
fn color_path(id) { panel_path("color-panel." + id) }
fn size_path(id) { panel_path("size-panel." + id) }
fn shape_path(id) { panel_path("shape-panel." + id) }
fn renderer_path(id) { panel_path("renderer-panel." + id) }
fn curves_path(id) { panel_path("curves-panel." + id) }
fn motion_path(id) { panel_path("motion-panel." + id) }
fn forces_path(id) { panel_path("forces-panel." + id) }
fn spawn_path(id) { panel_path("spawn-panel." + id) }
fn burst_path(id) { panel_path("burst-panel." + id) }
fn export_path(id) { panel_path("export-panel." + id) }

fn spawn_rate(world_api) { world_api.state.get_float("spawn_rate") }
fn max_particles(world_api) { world_api.state.get_int("max_particles") }
fn lifetime(world_api) { world_api.state.get_float("lifetime") }
fn speed(world_api) { world_api.state.get_float("speed") }
fn spread(world_api) { world_api.state.get_float("spread") }
fn initial_size(world_api) { world_api.state.get_float("initial_size") }
fn final_size(world_api) { world_api.state.get_float("final_size") }
fn gravity_y(world_api) { world_api.state.get_float("gravity_y") }
fn drag(world_api) { world_api.state.get_float("drag") }
fn active(world_api) { world_api.state.get_bool("active") }
fn color_name(world_api) { world_api.state.get_string("color") }
fn ramp_preset(world_api) { world_api.state.get_string("ramp_preset") }
fn ramp_interpolation(world_api) { world_api.state.get_string("ramp_interpolation") }
fn selected_tab(world_api) { world_api.state.get_string("selected_tab") }
fn spawn_kind(world_api) { world_api.state.get_string("spawn_kind") }
fn shape_mode(world_api) { world_api.state.get_string("shape_mode") }
fn shape_kind(world_api) { world_api.state.get_string("shape_kind") }
fn align_kind(world_api) { world_api.state.get_string("align_kind") }
fn blend_kind(world_api) { world_api.state.get_string("blend_kind") }
fn z_index(world_api) { world_api.state.get_float("z_index") }
fn selected_preset(world_api) { world_api.state.get_string("selected_preset") }

fn lerp(min, max, value) {
    return min + (max - min) * value;
}

fn normalize(min, max, value) {
    return (value - min) / (max - min);
}

fn bool_text(value) {
    if value { return "true"; }
    return "false";
}

fn export_id(world_api) {
    let preset = selected_preset(world_api);
    if preset == "" {
        return "editor-export";
    }
    return preset + "-edited";
}

fn export_label(world_api) {
    let label = world_api.particles.preset_label(selected_preset(world_api));
    if label == "" {
        return "Editor Export";
    }
    return label + " Edited";
}

fn export_category(world_api) {
    let category = world_api.particles.preset_category(selected_preset(world_api));
    if category == "" {
        return "custom";
    }
    return category;
}

fn export_tags(world_api) {
    let tags = world_api.particles.preset_tags(selected_preset(world_api));
    if tags == "" {
        return "[editor, custom]";
    }
    return "[" + tags + ", editor, edited]";
}

fn color_hex(name) {
    if name == "Orange" { return "#FF8A22FF"; }
    if name == "White" { return "#F8F8F8FF"; }
    if name == "Purple" { return "#7E5BFFFF"; }
    if name == "Smoke" { return "#A0A0A066"; }
    return "#39D7FFFF";
}

fn pipe_join(values) {
    let output = "";
    let index = 0;
    while index < values.len() {
        if index > 0 {
            output += "|";
        }
        output += values[index];
        index += 1;
    }
    return output;
}

fn apply_color(world_api, name) {
    if name == "Orange" {
        world_api.particles.set_color_rgba(emitter(), 1.0, 0.54, 0.13, 0.88);
        return;
    }
    if name == "White" {
        world_api.particles.set_color_rgba(emitter(), 0.97, 0.97, 0.97, 1.0);
        return;
    }
    if name == "Purple" {
        world_api.particles.set_color_rgba(emitter(), 0.49, 0.36, 1.0, 1.0);
        return;
    }
    if name == "Smoke" {
        world_api.particles.set_color_rgba(emitter(), 0.63, 0.63, 0.63, 0.40);
        return;
    }
    world_api.particles.set_color_rgba(emitter(), 0.22, 0.84, 1.0, 1.0);
}

fn ramp_stop_color(preset, index) {
    if preset == "Fire" {
        if index == 0 { return "#FFFFFFFF"; }
        if index == 1 { return "#FFE680FF"; }
        if index == 2 { return "#FF5A1FCC"; }
        return "#30100000";
    }
    if preset == "Smoke" {
        if index == 0 { return "#40405000"; }
        if index == 1 { return "#A0A0A066"; }
        if index == 2 { return "#C8C8D055"; }
        return "#20202800";
    }
    if preset == "Plasma" {
        if index == 0 { return "#FFFFFFFF"; }
        if index == 1 { return "#39D7FFFF"; }
        if index == 2 { return "#236DFFFF"; }
        return "#0A1A6600";
    }
    if preset == "Portal" {
        if index == 0 { return "#7E5BFF00"; }
        if index == 1 { return "#7E5BFFFF"; }
        if index == 2 { return "#39D7FFFF"; }
        return "#F8F8F800";
    }
    if preset == "Snow" {
        if index == 0 { return "#F8F8F8FF"; }
        if index == 1 { return "#DDEBFFFF"; }
        if index == 2 { return "#BFD7FFFF"; }
        return "#F8F8F800";
    }
    return "#39D7FFFF";
}

fn apply_color_ramp(world_api) {
    let preset = ramp_preset(world_api);
    if preset == "None" || preset == "" {
        world_api.particles.clear_color_ramp(emitter());
        apply_color(world_api, color_name(world_api));
        return;
    }
    world_api.particles.set_color_ramp4(
        emitter(),
        ramp_interpolation(world_api),
        0.0, ramp_stop_color(preset, 0),
        0.25, ramp_stop_color(preset, 1),
        0.70, ramp_stop_color(preset, 2),
        1.0, ramp_stop_color(preset, 3)
    );
}

fn apply_params(world_api) {
    world_api.particles.set_active(emitter(), active(world_api));
    world_api.particles.set_spawn_rate(emitter(), spawn_rate(world_api));
    world_api.particles.set_max_particles(emitter(), max_particles(world_api));
    world_api.particles.set_lifetime(emitter(), lifetime(world_api));
    world_api.particles.set_speed(emitter(), speed(world_api));
    world_api.particles.set_spread_degrees(emitter(), spread(world_api));
    world_api.particles.set_initial_size(emitter(), initial_size(world_api));
    world_api.particles.set_final_size(emitter(), final_size(world_api));
    world_api.particles.set_gravity(emitter(), 0.0, gravity_y(world_api));
    world_api.particles.set_drag(emitter(), drag(world_api));
    if spawn_kind(world_api) == "point" {
        world_api.particles.set_spawn_area_point(emitter());
    } else if spawn_kind(world_api) == "line" {
        world_api.particles.set_spawn_area_line(emitter(), 120.0);
    } else if spawn_kind(world_api) == "ring" {
        world_api.particles.set_spawn_area_ring(emitter(), 24.0, 52.0);
    } else {
        world_api.particles.set_spawn_area_circle(emitter(), 8.0);
    }
    if shape_mode(world_api) == "random_mix" {
        world_api.particles.set_shape_mix(emitter(), 2.0, 1.0, 1.0);
    } else {
        if shape_kind(world_api) == "line" {
            world_api.particles.set_shape_line(emitter(), 14.0);
        } else if shape_kind(world_api) == "quad" {
            world_api.particles.set_shape_quad(emitter());
        } else {
            world_api.particles.set_shape_circle(emitter(), 8);
        }
    }
    world_api.particles.set_align(emitter(), align_kind(world_api));
    world_api.particles.set_blend_mode(emitter(), blend_kind(world_api));
    world_api.particles.set_z_index(emitter(), z_index(world_api));
    apply_color_ramp(world_api);
}

fn sync_model_state(world_api) {
    let preset_label = world_api.particles.preset_label(selected_preset(world_api));
    let preset_category = world_api.particles.preset_category(selected_preset(world_api));
    let preset_tags = world_api.particles.preset_tags(selected_preset(world_api));
    world_api.state.set_string("active_theme", "space_dark");
    world_api.state.set_string("preset_options", pipe_join(world_api.particles.preset_ids()));
    world_api.state.set_string("preset_meta", "Preset: " + preset_label + " / " + preset_category + " / " + preset_tags);
    world_api.state.set_string("tab_title", selected_tab(world_api) + " controls");
    world_api.state.set_float("active_value", if active(world_api) { 1.0 } else { 0.0 });
    world_api.state.set_float("spawn_rate_normalized", normalize(0.0, 200.0, spawn_rate(world_api)));
    world_api.state.set_float("max_particles_normalized", normalize(16.0, 320.0, max_particles(world_api)));
    world_api.state.set_float("lifetime_normalized", normalize(0.1, 1.8, lifetime(world_api)));
    world_api.state.set_float("speed_normalized", normalize(0.0, 300.0, speed(world_api)));
    world_api.state.set_float("spread_normalized", normalize(0.0, 180.0, spread(world_api)));
    world_api.state.set_float("initial_size_normalized", normalize(0.0, 20.0, initial_size(world_api)));
    world_api.state.set_float("final_size_normalized", normalize(0.0, 20.0, final_size(world_api)));
    world_api.state.set_float("z_index_normalized", normalize(-20.0, 80.0, z_index(world_api)));
    world_api.state.set_float("gravity_y_normalized", normalize(-300.0, 300.0, gravity_y(world_api)));
    world_api.state.set_float("drag_normalized", normalize(0.0, 5.0, drag(world_api)));
    world_api.state.set_string("color_swatch", color_hex(color_name(world_api)));
    world_api.state.set_string("ramp_stop_0", ramp_stop_color(ramp_preset(world_api), 0));
    world_api.state.set_string("ramp_stop_1", ramp_stop_color(ramp_preset(world_api), 1));
    world_api.state.set_string("ramp_stop_2", ramp_stop_color(ramp_preset(world_api), 2));
    world_api.state.set_string("ramp_stop_3", ramp_stop_color(ramp_preset(world_api), 3));
    world_api.state.set_string("spawn_rate_label", "Spawn rate " + spawn_rate(world_api).to_int());
    world_api.state.set_string("max_particles_label", "Max particles " + max_particles(world_api));
    world_api.state.set_string("lifetime_label", "Lifetime " + lifetime(world_api));
    world_api.state.set_string("speed_label", "Speed " + speed(world_api).to_int());
    world_api.state.set_string("spread_label", "Spread " + spread(world_api).to_int());
    world_api.state.set_string("size_label", "Size " + initial_size(world_api).to_int() + " -> " + final_size(world_api).to_int());
    world_api.state.set_string("shape_label", "Shape " + shape_kind(world_api) + " / Mode " + shape_mode(world_api) + " / Align " + align_kind(world_api));
    world_api.state.set_string("renderer_label", "Blend " + blend_kind(world_api) + " / z-index " + z_index(world_api).to_int());
    world_api.state.set_string("gravity_label", "Gravity Y " + gravity_y(world_api).to_int());
    world_api.state.set_string("drag_label", "Drag " + drag(world_api));
    world_api.state.set_string("spawn_label", "Spawn area " + spawn_kind(world_api));
    world_api.state.set_string("ramp_label", "ColorRamp " + ramp_preset(world_api) + " / " + ramp_interpolation(world_api));

    let tab = selected_tab(world_api);
    world_api.state.set_bool("tab_emission_visible", tab == "Emission");
    world_api.state.set_bool("tab_color_visible", tab == "Color");
    world_api.state.set_bool("tab_size_visible", tab == "Size");
    world_api.state.set_bool("tab_shape_visible", tab == "Shape");
    world_api.state.set_bool("tab_renderer_visible", tab == "Renderer");
    world_api.state.set_bool("tab_curves_visible", tab == "Curves");
    world_api.state.set_bool("tab_motion_visible", tab == "Motion");
    world_api.state.set_bool("tab_forces_visible", tab == "Forces");
    world_api.state.set_bool("tab_spawn_visible", tab == "Spawn");
    world_api.state.set_bool("tab_burst_visible", tab == "Burst");
    world_api.state.set_bool("tab_export_visible", tab == "Export");
}

fn sync_tabs(world_api) {
    let tab = selected_tab(world_api);
    if tab == "" {
        tab = "Emission";
        world_api.state.set_string("selected_tab", tab);
    }
    sync_model_state(world_api);
}

fn set_tab(world_api, tab) {
    world_api.state.set_string("selected_tab", tab);
    sync_tabs(world_api);
}

fn apply_curve_payload(world_api, curve_name, payload) {
    if payload.len() < 8 {
        return;
    }
    world_api.particles.set_curve4(
        emitter(),
        curve_name,
        payload[4].to_float(),
        payload[5].to_float(),
        payload[6].to_float(),
        payload[7].to_float()
    );
    world_api.state.set_string(curve_name + "_curve_label", curve_name + " curve " + payload[3]);
}

fn reset_params(world_api) {
    world_api.state.set_bool("active", true);
    world_api.state.set_float("spawn_rate", 90.0);
    world_api.state.set_int("max_particles", 160);
    world_api.state.set_float("lifetime", 0.65);
    world_api.state.set_float("speed", 100.0);
    world_api.state.set_float("spread", 28.0);
    world_api.state.set_float("initial_size", 4.0);
    world_api.state.set_float("final_size", 13.0);
    world_api.state.set_float("gravity_y", 0.0);
    world_api.state.set_float("drag", 0.4);
    world_api.state.set_string("color", "Cyan");
    world_api.state.set_string("ramp_preset", "Plasma");
    world_api.state.set_string("ramp_interpolation", "linear_rgb");
    world_api.state.set_string("spawn_kind", "circle");
    world_api.state.set_string("shape_mode", "single");
    world_api.state.set_string("shape_kind", "circle");
    world_api.state.set_string("align_kind", "velocity");
    world_api.state.set_string("blend_kind", "alpha");
    world_api.state.set_float("z_index", 24.0);
    world_api.state.set_string("selected_preset", "plasma");
    world_api.state.set_string("selected_tab", "Emission");
    world_api.state.set_string("emission_curve_label", "Emission rate");
    world_api.state.set_string("size_curve_label", "Size over lifetime");
    world_api.state.set_string("alpha_curve_label", "Alpha over lifetime");
    world_api.state.set_string("speed_curve_label", "Speed over intensity");
    apply_params(world_api);
    sync_tabs(world_api);
}

fn export_yaml_text(world_api) {
    let yaml = "";
    yaml += "kind: particle-preset-2d\n";
    yaml += "id: " + export_id(world_api) + "\n";
    yaml += "label: " + export_label(world_api) + "\n";
    yaml += "category: " + export_category(world_api) + "\n";
    yaml += "tags: " + export_tags(world_api) + "\n";
    yaml += "emitter:\n";
    yaml += "  type: ParticleEmitter2D\n";
    yaml += "  active: " + bool_text(active(world_api)) + "\n";
    yaml += "  spawn_rate: " + spawn_rate(world_api) + "\n";
    yaml += "  max_particles: " + max_particles(world_api) + "\n";
    yaml += "  particle_lifetime: " + lifetime(world_api) + "\n";
    yaml += "  lifetime_jitter: 0.08\n";
    yaml += "  initial_speed: " + speed(world_api) + "\n";
    yaml += "  speed_jitter: 24.0\n";
    yaml += "  spread_degrees: " + spread(world_api) + "\n";
    yaml += "  initial_size: " + initial_size(world_api) + "\n";
    yaml += "  final_size: " + final_size(world_api) + "\n";
    yaml += "  color: \"" + color_hex(color_name(world_api)) + "\"\n";
    if ramp_preset(world_api) != "None" {
        yaml += "  color_ramp:\n";
        yaml += "    interpolation: " + ramp_interpolation(world_api) + "\n";
        yaml += "    stops:\n";
        yaml += "      - { t: 0.0, color: \"" + ramp_stop_color(ramp_preset(world_api), 0) + "\" }\n";
        yaml += "      - { t: 0.25, color: \"" + ramp_stop_color(ramp_preset(world_api), 1) + "\" }\n";
        yaml += "      - { t: 0.70, color: \"" + ramp_stop_color(ramp_preset(world_api), 2) + "\" }\n";
        yaml += "      - { t: 1.0, color: \"" + ramp_stop_color(ramp_preset(world_api), 3) + "\" }\n";
    }
    yaml += "  forces:\n";
    yaml += "    - kind: gravity\n";
    yaml += "      acceleration: { x: 0.0, y: " + gravity_y(world_api) + " }\n";
    yaml += "    - kind: drag\n";
    yaml += "      coefficient: " + drag(world_api) + "\n";
    yaml += "  spawn_area:\n";
    yaml += "    kind: " + spawn_kind(world_api) + "\n";
    if spawn_kind(world_api) == "line" {
        yaml += "    length: 120.0\n";
    } else if spawn_kind(world_api) == "ring" {
        yaml += "    inner_radius: 24.0\n";
        yaml += "    outer_radius: 52.0\n";
    } else if spawn_kind(world_api) == "circle" {
        yaml += "    radius: 8.0\n";
    }
    yaml += "  shape:\n";
    yaml += "    kind: " + shape_kind(world_api) + "\n";
    if shape_kind(world_api) == "circle" {
        yaml += "    segments: 8\n";
    } else if shape_kind(world_api) == "line" {
        yaml += "    length: 14.0\n";
    }
    if shape_mode(world_api) == "random_mix" {
        yaml += "  shape_choices:\n";
        yaml += "    - weight: 2.0\n";
        yaml += "      shape: { kind: circle, segments: 8 }\n";
        yaml += "    - weight: 1.0\n";
        yaml += "      shape: { kind: line, length: 14.0 }\n";
        yaml += "    - weight: 1.0\n";
        yaml += "      shape: { kind: quad }\n";
    }
    yaml += "  align: " + align_kind(world_api) + "\n";
    yaml += "  blend_mode: " + blend_kind(world_api) + "\n";
    yaml += "  z_index: " + z_index(world_api) + "\n";
    yaml += "  emission_rate_curve: { kind: constant, value: 1.0 }\n";
    yaml += "  size_curve: { kind: ease_out }\n";
    yaml += "  alpha_curve:\n";
    yaml += "    kind: custom\n";
    yaml += "    points:\n";
    yaml += "      - { t: 0.0, value: 0.90 }\n";
    yaml += "      - { t: 0.70, value: 0.45 }\n";
    yaml += "      - { t: 1.0, value: 0.0 }\n";
    yaml += "  speed_curve: { kind: ease_out }\n";
    return yaml;
}

fn print_yaml(world_api) {
    let yaml = export_yaml_text(world_api);
    world_api.dev.log("--- particle preset export begin ---");
    world_api.dev.log(yaml);
    world_api.dev.write_text("particle-presets/" + export_id(world_api) + ".yml", yaml);
    world_api.dev.log("--- particle preset export end ---");
}

fn on_enter() {
    world.state.reset_scene();
    reset_params(world);
}

fn update(dt) {}

fn on_event(topic, payload) {
    if topic == "playground-2d-particles.editor.active" {
        world.state.set_bool("active", payload[0].to_float() >= 0.5);
        apply_params(world);
        sync_model_state(world);
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-2d-particles.editor.tab" {
        set_tab(world, payload[0]);
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-2d-particles.editor.preset" {
        world.state.set_string("selected_preset", payload[0]);
        world.particles.apply_preset(payload[0], emitter());
        world.particles.set_active(emitter(), active(world));
        sync_model_state(world);
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-2d-particles.editor.spawn-rate" {
        world.state.set_float("spawn_rate", lerp(0.0, 200.0, payload[0].to_float()));
        apply_params(world);
        sync_model_state(world);
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-2d-particles.editor.max-particles" {
        world.state.set_int("max_particles", lerp(16.0, 320.0, payload[0].to_float()).to_int());
        apply_params(world);
        sync_model_state(world);
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-2d-particles.editor.lifetime" {
        world.state.set_float("lifetime", lerp(0.1, 1.8, payload[0].to_float()));
        apply_params(world);
        sync_model_state(world);
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-2d-particles.editor.speed" {
        world.state.set_float("speed", lerp(0.0, 300.0, payload[0].to_float()));
        apply_params(world);
        sync_model_state(world);
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-2d-particles.editor.spread" {
        world.state.set_float("spread", lerp(0.0, 180.0, payload[0].to_float()));
        apply_params(world);
        sync_model_state(world);
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-2d-particles.editor.initial-size" {
        world.state.set_float("initial_size", lerp(0.0, 20.0, payload[0].to_float()));
        apply_params(world);
        sync_model_state(world);
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-2d-particles.editor.final-size" {
        world.state.set_float("final_size", lerp(0.0, 20.0, payload[0].to_float()));
        apply_params(world);
        sync_model_state(world);
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-2d-particles.editor.shape-kind" {
        world.state.set_string("shape_kind", payload[0]);
        apply_params(world);
        sync_model_state(world);
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-2d-particles.editor.shape-mode" {
        world.state.set_string("shape_mode", payload[0]);
        apply_params(world);
        sync_model_state(world);
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-2d-particles.editor.align-kind" {
        world.state.set_string("align_kind", payload[0]);
        apply_params(world);
        sync_model_state(world);
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-2d-particles.editor.blend-kind" {
        world.state.set_string("blend_kind", payload[0]);
        apply_params(world);
        sync_model_state(world);
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-2d-particles.editor.z-index" {
        world.state.set_float("z_index", lerp(-20.0, 80.0, payload[0].to_float()));
        apply_params(world);
        sync_model_state(world);
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-2d-particles.editor.curve-emission" {
        apply_curve_payload(world, "emission", payload);
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-2d-particles.editor.curve-size" {
        apply_curve_payload(world, "size", payload);
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-2d-particles.editor.curve-alpha" {
        apply_curve_payload(world, "alpha", payload);
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-2d-particles.editor.curve-speed" {
        apply_curve_payload(world, "speed", payload);
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-2d-particles.editor.gravity-y" {
        world.state.set_float("gravity_y", lerp(-300.0, 300.0, payload[0].to_float()));
        apply_params(world);
        sync_model_state(world);
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-2d-particles.editor.drag" {
        world.state.set_float("drag", lerp(0.0, 5.0, payload[0].to_float()));
        apply_params(world);
        sync_model_state(world);
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-2d-particles.editor.color" {
        world.state.set_string("color", payload[0]);
        world.state.set_string("ramp_preset", "None");
        apply_params(world);
        sync_model_state(world);
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-2d-particles.editor.ramp-preset" {
        world.state.set_string("ramp_preset", payload[0]);
        apply_params(world);
        sync_model_state(world);
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-2d-particles.editor.ramp-interpolation" {
        world.state.set_string("ramp_interpolation", payload[0]);
        apply_params(world);
        sync_model_state(world);
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-2d-particles.editor.rgb-color" {
        world.state.set_string("ramp_preset", "None");
        world.particles.set_color_rgba(
            emitter(),
            payload[0].to_float(),
            payload[1].to_float(),
            payload[2].to_float(),
            1.0
        );
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-2d-particles.editor.spawn-kind" {
        world.state.set_string("spawn_kind", payload[0]);
        apply_params(world);
        sync_model_state(world);
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-2d-particles.editor.reset" {
        reset_params(world);
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-2d-particles.editor.print-yaml" {
        print_yaml(world);
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-2d-particles.editor.burst" {
        world.particles.burst(emitter(), 48);
        return;
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: mods/playground-2d-particles/scenes/editor/scene.yml
====================================================================================================
version: 1
scene:
  id: editor
  label: Particle Editor
  description: Runtime ParticleEmitter2D parameter editing with first UI controls.

entities:
  - id: controls
    name: playground-2d-particles-editor-controls
    components:
      - type: InputActionMap
        id: editor
        active: true
        actions:
          ui.back:
            kind: button
            pressed: [Escape]
      - type: EventPipeline
        id: back-to-menu
        topic: playground-2d-particles.menu
        steps:
          - kind: transition_scene
            scene: menu
      - type: Behavior
        kind: scene_back_controller
        action: ui.back
        scene: menu

  - id: camera

====================================================================================================
FILE: mods/playground-2d-particles/scenes/showcase/scene.rhai
====================================================================================================
import "pkg:amigo.std/input" as input;

fn root() { "playground-2d-particles-showcase-ui.root" }
fn path(id) { root() + "." + id }
fn controls_path(id) { path("body.side." + id) }
fn preview_emitter() { "playground-2d-particles-preview-emitter" }

fn selected_preset(world_api) { world_api.state.get_string("selected_preset") }
fn intensity(world_api) { world_api.state.get_float("intensity") }
fn preview_active(world_api) { world_api.state.get_bool("preview_active") }

fn percent(value) {
    return (value * 100.0).to_int() + "%";
}

fn pipe_join(values) {
    let output = "";
    let index = 0;
    while index < values.len() {
        if index > 0 {
            output += "|";
        }
        output += values[index];
        index += 1;
    }
    return output;
}

fn is_burst_preset(world_api, preset) {
    if preset == "explosion" { return true; }
    if preset == "shockwave" { return true; }
    if preset == "muzzle_flash" { return true; }
    return world_api.particles.preset_category(preset) == "burst";
}

fn sync_model_state(world_api) {
    let preset = selected_preset(world_api);
    let label = world_api.particles.preset_label(preset);
    let category = world_api.particles.preset_category(preset);
    let tags = world_api.particles.preset_tags(preset);
    world_api.state.set_string("active_theme", "space_dark");
    world_api.state.set_string("preset_options", pipe_join(world_api.particles.preset_ids()));
    world_api.state.set_string("selected_preset_label", "Selected: " + preset);
    world_api.state.set_string("intensity_label", "Intensity " + percent(intensity(world_api)));
    world_api.state.set_string("current_preset_detail", label + " / " + category + " / " + tags);
}

fn focus_selected(world_api) {
    let preset = selected_preset(world_api);
    world_api.particles.apply_preset(preset, preview_emitter());
    world_api.particles.set_intensity(preview_emitter(), intensity(world_api));
    world_api.particles.set_active(preview_emitter(), preview_active(world_api) && !is_burst_preset(world_api, preset));
    if is_burst_preset(world_api, preset) {
        world_api.particles.burst(preview_emitter(), 96);
    }
}

fn set_selected_preset(world_api, preset) {
    world_api.state.set_string("selected_preset", preset);
    focus_selected(world_api);
    sync_model_state(world_api);
}

fn set_selected_intensity(world_api, value) {
    let clamped = value;
    if clamped < 0.0 { clamped = 0.0; }
    if clamped > 1.0 { clamped = 1.0; }
    world_api.state.set_float("intensity", clamped);
    world_api.particles.set_intensity(preview_emitter(), clamped);
    sync_model_state(world_api);
}

fn set_all_active(world_api, active) {
    world_api.state.set_bool("preview_active", active);
    world_api.particles.set_active(preview_emitter(), active && !is_burst_preset(world_api, selected_preset(world_api)));
}

fn toggle_selected(world_api) {
    set_all_active(world_api, !preview_active(world_api));
}

fn on_enter() {
    world.state.reset_scene();
    world.state.set_string("selected_preset", "fire");
    world.state.set_float("intensity", 1.0);
    world.state.set_bool("preview_active", true);

    focus_selected(world);
    sync_model_state(world);
}

fn update(dt) {
    if input::pressed(world, "showcase.select_fire") { set_selected_preset(world, "fire"); }
    if input::pressed(world, "showcase.select_smoke") { set_selected_preset(world, "smoke"); }
    if input::pressed(world, "showcase.select_sparks") { set_selected_preset(world, "sparks"); }
    if input::pressed(world, "showcase.select_magic") { set_selected_preset(world, "magic"); }
    if input::pressed(world, "showcase.select_snow") { set_selected_preset(world, "snow"); }
    if input::pressed(world, "showcase.select_dust") { set_selected_preset(world, "dust"); }
    if input::pressed(world, "showcase.select_engine_plume") { set_selected_preset(world, "engine_plume"); }
    if input::pressed(world, "showcase.select_plasma") { set_selected_preset(world, "plasma"); }
    if input::pressed(world, "showcase.select_portal") { set_selected_preset(world, "portal"); }
    if input::pressed(world, "showcase.select_explosion") { set_selected_preset(world, "explosion"); }

    if input::pressed(world, "showcase.toggle_selected") {
        toggle_selected(world);
    }
    if input::pressed(world, "showcase.start_all") {
        set_all_active(world, true);
    }
    if input::pressed(world, "showcase.stop_all") {
        set_all_active(world, false);
    }
    if input::pressed(world, "showcase.reset") {
        set_selected_intensity(world, 1.0);
    }
    if input::pressed(world, "showcase.burst") {
        world.particles.burst(preview_emitter(), 48);
    }
}

fn on_event(topic, payload) {
    if topic == "playground-2d-particles.showcase.start-all" {
        set_all_active(world, true);
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-2d-particles.showcase.stop-all" {
        set_all_active(world, false);
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-2d-particles.showcase.select" {
        set_selected_preset(world, payload[0]);
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-2d-particles.showcase.intensity" {
        set_selected_intensity(world, payload[0].to_float());
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-2d-particles.showcase.intensity-reset" {
        set_selected_intensity(world, 1.0);
        return;
    }
}

FILE: mods/playground-2d-particles/scenes/showcase/scene.yml
====================================================================================================
version: 1
scene:

====================================================================================================
FILE: mods/playground-3d/scenes/hello-world-cube/scene.rhai
====================================================================================================
let cube = world.entities.named("playground-3d-cube");

fn on_enter() {
    let scene_id = world.scene.current_id();
    world.dev.event("playground-3d.scene-ready", scene_id);
    world.dev.log("playground-3d scene bootstrapped from scene document for scene=" + scene_id);
}

fn update(dt) {
    let speed = 1.0 / 3.0;
    cube.rotate_3d(speed * dt, speed * 0.67 * dt, speed * 1.31 * dt);
}
