====================================================================================================
FILE: mods/playground-hud-ui/scenes/showcase/scene.rhai
====================================================================================================
import "pkg:amigo.std/input" as input;

fn root() { "playground-hud-ui-showcase.root" }
fn path(id) { root() + "." + id }
fn controls_path(id) { path("main.controls." + id) }
fn preview_path(id) { path("main.preview." + id) }
fn editor_path(id) { path("main.editor." + id) }

fn percent(value) {
    let whole = (value * 100.0).to_int();
    return whole + "%";
}

fn active_theme(world_api) { world_api.state.get_string("active_theme") }
fn volume(world_api) { world_api.state.get_float("volume") }
fn spawn_rate(world_api) { world_api.state.get_float("spawn_rate") }
fn lifetime(world_api) { world_api.state.get_float("lifetime") }
fn debug_enabled(world_api) { world_api.state.get_bool("debug_enabled") }
fn color_preset(world_api) { world_api.state.get_string("color_preset") }
fn selected_tab(world_api) { world_api.state.get_string("selected_tab") }

fn color_hex(name) {
    if name == "Blue" { return "#236DFFFF"; }
    if name == "Orange" { return "#FF8A22FF"; }
    if name == "White" { return "#F8F8F8FF"; }
    return "#7E5BFFFF";
}

fn sync_model_state(world_api) {
    world_api.state.set_string("active_theme_label", "Theme: " + active_theme(world_api));
    world_api.state.set_string("volume_label", "Volume " + percent(volume(world_api)));
    world_api.state.set_string("debug_label", if debug_enabled(world_api) { "Debug overlay ON" } else { "Debug overlay OFF" });
    world_api.state.set_string("selected_tab_label", "Active tab: " + selected_tab(world_api));
    world_api.state.set_string("spawn_rate_label", "Spawn rate " + percent(spawn_rate(world_api)));
    world_api.state.set_string("lifetime_label", "Lifetime " + percent(lifetime(world_api)));
    world_api.state.set_string("color_preset_label", "Color preset " + color_preset(world_api));
    world_api.state.set_string("color_swatch", color_hex(color_preset(world_api)));
    world_api.state.set_string("debug_text", "selected_theme=" + active_theme(world_api) + " selected_tab=" + selected_tab(world_api) + " volume=" + percent(volume(world_api)) + " spawn_rate=" + percent(spawn_rate(world_api)));
}

fn set_theme(world_api, theme) {
    world_api.state.set_string("active_theme", theme);
    world_api.ui.set_text(path("toast.message"), "Theme changed: " + theme);
    world_api.ui.show(path("toast"));
    sync_model_state(world_api);
}

fn clamp01(value) {
    if value < 0.0 { return 0.0; }
    if value > 1.0 { return 1.0; }
    return value;
}

fn on_enter() {
    world.state.reset_scene();
    world.state.set_string("active_theme", "space_dark");
    world.state.set_float("volume", 0.65);
    world.state.set_float("spawn_rate", 0.72);
    world.state.set_float("lifetime", 0.42);
    world.state.set_bool("debug_enabled", true);
    world.state.set_string("color_preset", "Blue");
    world.state.set_string("selected_tab", "Color");
    sync_model_state(world);
}

fn update(dt) {
    if input::pressed(world, "ui.theme.space_dark") {
        set_theme(world, "space_dark");
    }
    if input::pressed(world, "ui.theme.clean_dev") {
        set_theme(world, "clean_dev");
    }
    if input::pressed(world, "ui.theme.cycle") {
        if active_theme(world) == "space_dark" {
            set_theme(world, "clean_dev");
        } else {
            set_theme(world, "space_dark");
        }
    }
}

fn on_event(topic, payload) {
    if topic == "playground-hud-ui.theme.space-dark" {
        set_theme(world, "space_dark");
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-hud-ui.theme.clean-dev" {
        set_theme(world, "clean_dev");
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-hud-ui.volume.changed" {
        world.state.set_float("volume", payload[0].to_float());
        sync_model_state(world);
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-hud-ui.debug.changed" {
        world.state.set_bool("debug_enabled", payload[0].to_float() >= 0.5);
        sync_model_state(world);
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-hud-ui.spawn-rate.changed" {
        world.state.set_float("spawn_rate", payload[0].to_float());
        sync_model_state(world);
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-hud-ui.lifetime.changed" {
        world.state.set_float("lifetime", payload[0].to_float());
        sync_model_state(world);
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-hud-ui.color.changed" {
        world.state.set_string("color_preset", payload[0]);
        sync_model_state(world);
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-hud-ui.tab.changed" {
        world.state.set_string("selected_tab", payload[0]);
        sync_model_state(world);
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-hud-ui.stepper.minus" {
        world.state.set_float("volume", clamp01(volume(world) - 0.05));
        sync_model_state(world);
        return;
    }
    if topic == "playground-hud-ui.stepper.plus" {
        world.state.set_float("volume", clamp01(volume(world) + 0.05));
        sync_model_state(world);
        return;
    }
}

FILE: mods/playground-sidescroller/mod.toml
====================================================================================================
id = "playground-sidescroller"
name = "Sidescroller Demo"
version = "0.1.0"
description = "2D platformer-style vertical slice combining tilemaps, motion, physics, HUD, and audio."
authors = ["Amigo Engine Team"]
dependencies = ["core"]
capabilities = [
  "rendering_2d",
  "text_2d",
  "screen_space_ui",
  "physics_2d",
  "tilemap_2d",
  "motion_2d",
  "audio_api",
  "generated_audio",
]

[scripting]

====================================================================================================
FILE: mods/playground-sidescroller/README.md
====================================================================================================
# Sidescroller Demo

2D vertical-slice playground for Amigo. This mod is the first gameplay-oriented integration scene that combines:

- tilemap rendering
- kinematic platformer movement
- trigger events
- screen-space HUD
- generated audio
- camera follow

# Asset provenance

## Visual assets

### Pack: New Platformer Pack
- Source: https://kenney.nl/assets/new-platformer-pack
- Author: Kenney
- License: Creative Commons CC0 1.0 Universal
- Download date: 2026-04-28
- Original archive/file name: `kenney_new-platformer-pack-1.1.zip`
- Used files:
  - `Sprites/Characters/Default/character_beige_idle.png` -> `raw/images/player.png`
  - `Sprites/Characters/Default/character_beige_walk_a.png` -> `raw/images/player.png`
  - `Sprites/Characters/Default/character_beige_walk_b.png` -> `raw/images/player.png`
  - `Sprites/Characters/Default/character_beige_jump.png` -> `raw/images/player.png`
  - `Sprites/Tiles/Default/coin_gold.png` -> `raw/images/coin.png`
  - `Sprites/Tiles/Default/coin_gold_side.png` -> `raw/images/coin.png`
  - `Sprites/Tiles/Default/flag_green_b.png` -> `raw/images/finish.png`
  - `Sprites/Tiles/Default/terrain_grass_block.png` -> `raw/images/platformer.png`
  - `Sprites/Tiles/Default/terrain_grass_horizontal_left.png` -> `raw/images/platformer.png`
  - `Sprites/Tiles/Default/terrain_grass_horizontal_middle.png` -> `raw/images/platformer.png`
  - `Sprites/Tiles/Default/terrain_grass_horizontal_right.png` -> `raw/images/platformer.png`
  - `Sprites/Tiles/Default/terrain_grass_block_left.png` -> `raw/images/platformer.png`
  - `Sprites/Tiles/Default/terrain_grass_block_right.png` -> `raw/images/platformer.png`
  - `Sprites/Tiles/Default/terrain_grass_block_top.png` -> `raw/images/platformer.png`
  - `Sprites/Tiles/Default/terrain_grass_vertical_middle.png` -> `raw/images/platformer.png`
  - `Sprites/Tiles/Default/terrain_grass_block_bottom.png` -> `raw/images/platformer.png`
  - `Sprites/Tiles/Default/terrain_grass_block_top_left.png` -> `raw/images/platformer.png`
  - `Sprites/Tiles/Default/terrain_grass_block_top_right.png` -> `raw/images/platformer.png`
  - `Sprites/Tiles/Default/terrain_grass_block_bottom_left.png` -> `raw/images/platformer.png`
  - `Sprites/Tiles/Default/terrain_grass_block_bottom_right.png` -> `raw/images/platformer.png`
  - `Sprites/Tiles/Default/terrain_grass_block_center.png` -> `raw/images/platformer.png`
  - terrain rules metadata -> `spritesheets/platformer/rulesets/platform/rules.yml`
  - generator script -> `tools/generate-platformer-kit-assets.ps1`
- Modifications:
  - `raw/images/player.png`: repacked four character frames into a 4x1 spritesheet
  - `raw/images/coin.png`: repacked coin front/side sprites into a 4-frame loop
  - `raw/images/finish.png`: copied and renamed from the original flag sprite
  - `raw/images/platformer.png`: packed selected terrain tiles into an 18-tile atlas matching the current autotile resolver
  - metadata YAML added for `player`, `coin`, `finish`, `platformer`, and `platformer-rules`
- Notes:
  - only required files were copied into the repo
  - the downloaded archive itself is not committed
  - all imported visuals come from one primary pack to keep the style consistent
  - generated gameplay sprite and tileset outputs can be reproduced with `tools/generate-platformer-kit-assets.ps1`
  - `left_cap/right_cap/middle` are used for horizontal platform runs
  - `side_left/side_right/center` are used for wall sides and fully enclosed block interiors
  - the pack does not include explicit raster inner-corner tiles, so `inner_corner_*` currently fall back to `terrain_grass_block_center.png`

## Generated audio

Audio files in this mod are generated from metadata by Amigo generated audio systems.
No external SFX files are used.

Used generated audio definitions:
- `audio/jump/audio.yml`
- `audio/coin/audio.yml`
- `audio/hurt/audio.yml`
- `audio/level-complete/audio.yml`
- `audio/proximity-beep/audio.yml`

## Internal placeholders

- `raw/images/backgrounds/layer-01.png`
- `raw/images/backgrounds/layer-02.png`
- `raw/images/backgrounds/layer-03.png`
- `raw/images/backgrounds/layer-04.png`
- Created for Amigo playground-sidescroller using image generation
- License/ownership: internal project asset
- Purpose: custom numbered parallax background layers tuned for the current vertical-slice scene; the numbering is scene-level naming only, not an engine-imposed near/far restriction
- Layer layout:
  - `layer-01`: moonlit sky base
  - `layer-02`: distant forest silhouette overlay with alpha
  - `layer-03`: rolling hill overlay with alpha
  - `layer-04`: foreground foliage overlay with alpha
- `fonts/debug-ui/font.yml`
- Created for Amigo playground-sidescroller
- License/ownership: internal project placeholder metadata
- Purpose: keep the current debug UI font path stable until the font pipeline is upgraded to load a real font asset in this mod

FILE: mods/playground-sidescroller/scenes/vertical-slice/scene.rhai
====================================================================================================
import "pkg:amigo.std/input" as input;

let player = world.entities.named("playground-sidescroller-player");
let finish = world.entities.named("playground-sidescroller-finish");
let coin_names = [
    "playground-sidescroller-coin-01",
    "playground-sidescroller-coin-02",
    "playground-sidescroller-coin-03",
    "playground-sidescroller-coin-04",
    "playground-sidescroller-coin-05",
    "playground-sidescroller-coin-06",
    "playground-sidescroller-coin-07",
    "playground-sidescroller-coin-08",
    "playground-sidescroller-coin-09",
    "playground-sidescroller-coin-10",
    "playground-sidescroller-coin-11",
    "playground-sidescroller-coin-12",
    "playground-sidescroller-coin-13",
    "playground-sidescroller-coin-14",
    "playground-sidescroller-coin-15",
    "playground-sidescroller-coin-16",
    "playground-sidescroller-coin-17",
    "playground-sidescroller-coin-18",
    "playground-sidescroller-coin-19",
    "playground-sidescroller-coin-20",
    "playground-sidescroller-coin-21",
    "playground-sidescroller-coin-22",
    "playground-sidescroller-coin-23",
    "playground-sidescroller-coin-24",
    "playground-sidescroller-coin-25",
];
let total_coins = 25;
let lives = 3;
let score = 0;
let player_run_elapsed = 0.0;
let player_run_frame = 1;
let proximity_beep_active = false;
let last_proximity_distance = -1.0;
let proximity_beep_range = 960.0;
let proximity_beep_step = 96.0;
let last_time_seconds = -1;
let debug_elapsed = 0.0;
let last_debug_text = "";
let level_complete = false;
let collected_coins = [];

fn on_enter() {
    world.dev.log("playground-sidescroller vertical-slice bootstrapped");
    world.audio.preload("jump");
    world.audio.preload("coin");
    world.audio.preload("level-complete");
    world.audio.preload("proximity-beep");
    world.audio.play("jump");
    world.ui.set_text("playground-sidescroller-hud.root.lives", "Lives: " + lives);
    world.ui.set_text("playground-sidescroller-hud.root.time", "Time: 0s");
    world.ui.set_text("playground-sidescroller-hud.root.coins", "Coins: 0 / " + total_coins);
    world.ui.set_text(
        "playground-sidescroller-hud.root.debug",
        "grounded=false vx=0 vy=0 anim=idle",
    );
    world.ui.set_text("playground-sidescroller-hud.root.message", "READY");
    let proximity = world.entities.distance(player.name(), finish.name());
    level_complete = false;
    collected_coins = [];
    proximity_beep_active = false;
    last_proximity_distance = -1.0;
    if proximity >= 0.0 && proximity <= proximity_beep_range {
        world.audio.start_realtime("proximity-beep");
        world.audio.set_param("proximity-beep", "distance", proximity);
        proximity_beep_active = true;
        last_proximity_distance = proximity;
    }
    player_run_elapsed = 0.0;
    player_run_frame = 1;
    last_time_seconds = 0;
    debug_elapsed = 0.0;
    last_debug_text = "grounded=false vx=0 vy=0 anim=idle";
    world.sprite2d.set_frame(player.name(), 0);
    for coin_name in coin_names {
        world.sprite2d.set_frame(coin_name, 0);
    }
}

fn update(dt) {
    let move_x = input::axis(world, "player.move");
    let jump_pressed = input::pressed(world, "player.jump_pressed");
    let jump_held = input::down(world, "player.jump_down");

    let drove = world.motion.drive(player.name(), move_x, jump_pressed, jump_held, dt);
    let state = world.motion.state(player.name());
    let grounded = if state.grounded { "true" } else { "false" };
    let proximity = world.entities.distance(player.name(), finish.name());
    if level_complete {
        if proximity_beep_active {
            world.audio.stop("proximity-beep");
            proximity_beep_active = false;
            last_proximity_distance = -1.0;
        }
    } else if proximity >= 0.0 && proximity <= proximity_beep_range {
        if !proximity_beep_active {
            world.audio.start_realtime("proximity-beep");
            proximity_beep_active = true;
            last_proximity_distance = -1.0;
        }

        if last_proximity_distance < 0.0
            || proximity > last_proximity_distance + proximity_beep_step
            || proximity < last_proximity_distance - proximity_beep_step
        {
            world.audio.set_param("proximity-beep", "distance", proximity);
            last_proximity_distance = proximity;
        }
    } else if proximity_beep_active {
        world.audio.stop("proximity-beep");
        proximity_beep_active = false;
        last_proximity_distance = -1.0;
    }

    for coin_name in coin_names {
        world.sprite2d.advance(coin_name, dt);
    }

    if state.animation == "run" {
        player_run_elapsed = player_run_elapsed + dt;
        while player_run_elapsed >= 0.2 {
            player_run_elapsed = player_run_elapsed - 0.2;
            if player_run_frame == 1 {
                player_run_frame = 2;
            } else {
                player_run_frame = 1;
            }
        }
        world.sprite2d.set_frame(player.name(), player_run_frame);
    } else if state.animation == "jump" || state.animation == "fall" {
        player_run_elapsed = 0.0;
        player_run_frame = 1;
        world.sprite2d.set_frame(player.name(), 3);
    } else {
        player_run_elapsed = 0.0;
        player_run_frame = 1;
        world.sprite2d.set_frame(player.name(), 0);
    }

    let time_seconds = world.time.seconds();
    if time_seconds != last_time_seconds {
        world.ui.set_text(
            "playground-sidescroller-hud.root.time",
            "Time: " + time_seconds + "s",
        );
        last_time_seconds = time_seconds;
    }

    debug_elapsed = debug_elapsed + dt;
    if debug_elapsed >= 0.2 {
        debug_elapsed = 0.0;
        let debug_text =
            "grounded=" + grounded
                + " vx="
                + state.velocity_x_int
                + " vy="
                + state.velocity_y_int
                + " anim="
                + state.animation;
        if debug_text != last_debug_text {
            world.ui.set_text("playground-sidescroller-hud.root.debug", debug_text);
            last_debug_text = debug_text;
        }
    }
}

fn on_event(topic, payload) {
    if topic == "coin.collected" {
        if payload.len > 0 {
            let collected_coin = payload[0];
            for collected in collected_coins {
                if collected == collected_coin {
                    return;
                }
            }
            collected_coins.push(collected_coin);
        }

        score = score + 1;
        world.ui.set_text(
            "playground-sidescroller-hud.root.coins",
            "Coins: " + score + " / " + total_coins,
        );
        world.ui.set_text("playground-sidescroller-hud.root.message", "COIN COLLECTED");
        if payload.len > 0 {
            let collected_coin = payload[0];
            world.entities.set_position_2d(collected_coin, -10000.0, -10000.0);
        }
        world.audio.play("coin");
    }

    if topic == "player.jump" {
        world.ui.set_text("playground-sidescroller-hud.root.message", "JUMP");
        world.audio.play("jump");
    }

    if topic == "finish.reached" {
        if level_complete {
            return;
        }

        level_complete = true;
        world.ui.set_text("playground-sidescroller-hud.root.message", "LEVEL COMPLETE");
        world.audio.play("level-complete");
        world.audio.stop("proximity-beep");
        proximity_beep_active = false;
        last_proximity_distance = -1.0;
    }
}

scene:
  id: vertical-slice
  label: Sidescroller Vertical Slice

FILE: mods/they-are-rotten/scenes/main-menu/scene.rhai

fn seed_menu_state(world_api) {
    world_api.state.set_int("menu_index", 0);

    world_api.state.set_string("menu_color.0", "#39D7FFFF");
    world_api.state.set_string("menu_color.1", "#7D8796FF");
    world_api.state.set_string("menu_color.2", "#7D8796FF");

    world_api.state.set_string("menu_status", "READY");
    world_api.state.set_string("menu_status_color", "#7D8796FF");
}

fn on_enter() {
    world.state.reset_scene();
    seed_menu_state(world);
    world.dev.log("They Are Rotten main menu loaded.");
}

fn update(dt) {}

fn on_event(topic, payload) {
    if topic == "they-are-rotten.menu.start" {
        world.dev.log("Main Menu: START selected.");
        return;
    }

    if topic == "they-are-rotten.menu.options" {
        world.dev.log("Main Menu: OPTIONS selected.");
        return;
    }

    if topic == "they-are-rotten.menu.quit" {
        world.dev.log("Main Menu: QUIT selected.");
        return;
    }
}
